### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期，維持政策利率不變。決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂，也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對 12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在「通膨高企」與「經濟強韌」雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息，為歐元

提供部分支撐，但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國，在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下，歐元反彈力道相對有限。目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善，但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對 AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪；周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變，美元指數自 102
上方回落，加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐，境外人民幣突破 6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔，美中利差維持在相對高位，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號，USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50 
前週交易區間 
156.63 158.34

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息，升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、歐元區第三季 GDP
年增 1.3%，高於市場預期的 1.1%，顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場。此外，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%，優於市場預期的 3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破，顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1050-1.1270 
前週交易區間 
1.1103 1.1226

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.27-115.10.30 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 2.03% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.845%~1.846%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，時序邁入月底，銀行間已大致調整好積數，且央行未明顯
增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本週台債市場交投依舊清淡，10 年期指標券 A15109R 等殖系統成交在 2.03%，
5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。 
美債利率自波段高點回落，且央行總裁楊金龍本週立法院發言維持近來對通
膨容忍度變寬之態度，惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除，買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週 30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted Kinsus’ 3Q26 post-result follow-up call today (Nov 
10th). Key takeaways include: 
(1) Kinsus mentioned it is getting more market share from existing 
server CPU customers, AI GPU customer, and ASIC customers. 
Despite not having much market share gain opportunity in its AI 
server CPU products, the company mentioned the volume growth 
could still be 150% YoY in AI server CPU shipment in 2027 vs. 2026, 
suggesting a good revenue/GM growth outlook going into 2027/28.  
(2) Kinsus guides 2027/28 revenue to go up by at least 30% YoY 
each year while GM should be at least 35/40% in 2027/28, 
considering the orders/capacity/UTR/product mix differences, 
despite the key upside risk will be on like for like base pricing hike 
(the guidance above does not include any pricing hike assumption, 
but this could lift the revenue growth by another 15ppt in both 
years although not guaranteed, per the company). 
(3) For 4Q26, the company guided revenue to go up by 10% QoQ, 
with GM being expanded into 35% (from ~34% in 3Q26), despite a 
much more favorable than 3Q product mix and ~5-10% pricing hike 
on ABF & BT substrates. For 1Q27, the company expects some 
seasonal issues on consumer electronics and contact lens, and 
believes revenue should be flat to slightly up QoQ, despite possible 
GM slowdown during the low season. 
(4) In terms of the future investment plan, Kinsus mentioned it will 
only need to invest in the land/infrastructure/construction etc. while 
end customers will take care of the equipment; normally, equipment 
accounted for 60% of the total CAPEX while the rest only accounts 
for 40% of the total investment.  
(5) Kinsus mentioned it now holds ~70% of the AI server CPU 
substrate market share, and the company believes its market share 
should be pretty stable in this product cycle. For AI GPU, Kinsus

mentioned it expects its share to increase from 10% now to 30% in 2027 for its existing
customer’s supply chain. Kinsus also mentioned it is working on several ASIC customers’
substrates product, which could also be the key revenue/profit driver for 2027 and
beyond.
(6) In terms of the order forecast, the company mentioned it has not yet seen any order
cut or order delay on its ABF substrates business, but the company continues to
maintain its cautious but positive view on the overall substrate industry outlook.

Investment view
We are positive on Kinsus’ 3Q result (GM expanded by 7.8ppt QoQ in 3Q - see here), and
believe the company’s GM expansion trend will continue in coming quarters, despite the
company only guiding 1ppt QoQ growth on GM in 4Q (however, the company is also
giving a conservative guidance on profitability. Kinsus only guided GM to be 27-28% in
3Q26, but it ended up with 33.9% in its 3Q result). Moreover, we believe Kinsus’
proactive capacity expansion plan (1 new plant every year from 2025 to 2029) will start

to help the company to outperform peers from 2Q27 (the new K6-B plant starts
contributing revenue from 2Q27) which should also help the company to enjoy better
profitability in coming quarters, as the new plants will focus on high-end projects, whose
GM is generally 10ppt+ higher than the company’s average level).
Overall, we maintain our Buy rating on Kinsus, and we expect the better market share in
AI server CPU and its better position in the high-end ABF substrate industry to

strengthen into the long term.
We maintain our Buy rating with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,515, based on the
same 19.5x 2028E EPS, which is +2.0x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average
P/E (14.2x).
Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate

shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end
ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe

Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~118% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by a better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the
unfavorable demand outlook before 1H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,515 for Kinsus is based on
19.5x 2028E P/E, which is +2.0x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E

ab 10 November 2026
Global Research
All Ring Tech
Bright 2027 outlook with multiple drivers
Intact 2027 outlook
All Ring's 2027 outlook is robust, supported by three key drivers. 1) We expect TSMC
and ASE to continue accelerating CoWoS capacity expansion, with industry capacity
reaching ~270kwpm by end-2027E, up from ~160kwpm by end-2026. We estimate
TSMC and ASE’s capacity could reach ~180kwpm and ~70kwpm, respectively, by
2027E. 2) Second, with Intel's EMIB-T progressing on track toward volume production

by early 2028, we expect related equipment demand to begin ramping in 2027,
providing a new growth driver for All Ring. 3) CPO’s fiber array unit (FAU) coupling tools
start contributing to All Ring's 2027 revenue, with demand from Nvidia and AMD.
More concrete CPO development in 2027-28
Our industry feedback suggests Nvidia is taking the lead to aggressively push CPO mass
production in 2027 for scale up switch solution. AMD is also engaging with key

equipment suppliers, in preparation for competing CPO solution. Competition among
the equipment vendors is fierce, but we believe All Ring is well positioned in FAU
coupling as a tier 1 supplier, given the superior execution in driving higher tool
throughput versus peers. Considering the meaningful tool demand in 2027-28, we
forecast All Ring to recognize ~100 tools for revenue in 2027E, contributing ~12% of All
Ring's sales next year. Potential upside to our current estimates could come from larger

TAM for faster capacity expansion, All Ring securing higher share / ASP through better
tool performance.
Lower Q3 gross margin due to mix
Q3 EPS came in below LSEG consensus by -10% as gross margin of 48.5% missed
consensus of 54.2%. Lower gross margin could be due to a higher mix of automation
products, which carries a lower margin vs corporate average. For Q4, considering lighter
seasonality and current visibility, we model sales to decline by -28% from Q3 with gross

margin of 53% and EPS of NT$7.57.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and target of $1700
Post Q3 results, we fine-tune 2026 EPS by -4% due to lower gross margin in the quarter,
though our estimates for 2027/28 are largely kept unchanged. Our 2027E/2028E EPS
are 3%/30% ahead of Consensus pre-earnings. We maintain our Buy rating and price
target of $1700 based on 27x average 2027-28E PE.

Company Description
Founded in 1996, All Ring is a Taiwan-based supplier of automation equipment for the
semiconductor back-end packaging and passive component manufacturing industries. The
company's products include underfill dispensers, heatsink placement and automated optical
inspection (AOI) tools for advanced packaging equipment, particularly for heterogeneous

integration. Revenue is generated primarily from sales of semiconductor and electronic
manufacturing equipment and related automation solutions, with more than 90% of
revenue derived from Taiwan.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value All Ring based on PE methodology.
Risks for All Ring and the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in

the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development
such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech

companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in All Ring
gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from
either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in All
Ring losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could

lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to All
Ring, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of
the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact All Ring’s
margins for equipment shipments.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

relate to any product specific regulatory labelling regime or naming conventions. Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and
governance (ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of

ways. Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that
otherwise would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable
investing considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and

Hon Hai Precision  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 OpM beat; stay OW
Cloud:
• Management expects cloud  to rise above 60% of the mix in 3-5 years.
• Management expects to gain share in the next-generation Rubin platform, in
line with our view (report here).
• Many of Hon Hai's ASIC projects are on consignment (lower revenue but
higher GM). Some GPU projects are on consignment as well.
Company:
• Long-term OpM target remains 3%+. Management thinks it's achievable in

CY27 despite higher ASP of next-gen AI server racks.
•  Demand for key components will continue to outstrip supply in CY27, though
it should gradually ease as supply increases.
3Q results:
• Revenue: NT$3,015bn (+19% q/q, +46% y/y), driven by better-than-expected
AI server shipments and seasonality for smartphone products.
• GM: 6.0% (-10bps q/q, -30bps y/y), 20bps below our estimate/10bps below
the Street, affected by cost pressures and product mix.

• But opex ratio further declined to 2.4% (-20bps q/q) thanks to operating
leverage. Thus, OpM reached 3.8% (+10bps q/q, +40bps y/y), 20bps above
pour estimate/30bps above the Street.
• Non-op profit was higher than expected, so net profit reached NT$77.5bn
(+29% q/q, +34% y/y), 34% above our estimate/11% above the Street, for EPS
of NT$5.53.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

股價相對大盤走勢

大量(3167 TT) Buy

CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
投資建議：大量受惠於 AI 帶動 PCB 製造、半導體檢測需求，
獲利大幅成長，CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備：大量在±2 mil 
PCB 背鑽計量領域的地位依然穩固；大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶
動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備需求的趨勢，而非單純受惠某一客戶的拉
貨。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts： 大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億元，
QoQ+28.40%；稅後純益 8.99 億元，QoQ+65.51%；稅後 EPS 9.85
元。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts，主要來自產品組合優化，包含高階
CCD 機種及 TM3、 TM4。一般機種毛利率約 20~30%，高階機
種毛利率約 50%， TM3、 TM4 毛利率更高，TM4 於 3Q26 出貨
約 5 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：大量先進智能已經於
08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，預計大量
先進智能上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測
(Metrology)、 AOI 智慧檢測(Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。
產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、
CPO 等。

益預估大量 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 121.87 億元/182.81 億元，
YoY+140.00%/+50.00%；稅後純益 27.78 億元/56.60 億元，
YoY+287.95%/+103.72%；稅後 EPS 30.43 元 /62.00 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
E：(Environmental)。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持續推
動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低營運
活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持
續推動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低
營運活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技針對廠區內原物料、製程、生產活動
及服務建立環境考量評估機制，評估範圍涵蓋正常、異常及緊急狀況下可能
產生之環境影響，並納入空氣排放、水污染、廢棄物、能源使用、土地污染、
噪音及其他環境衝擊等項目。 
S：(Social)。大量科技重視人才發展與員工照顧，將員工視為公司穩健營運
與永續經營的重要基礎。大量科技秉持以人為本的精神，致力於培育與留任
專業人才，並透過多元招募、薪酬福利、教育訓練、勞資溝通及權益保障等
制度，持續打造友善、平等且具支持性的職場環境。大量科技於人才任用上
遵循相關勞動法令及多元平等原則，重視員工專業能力發展及工作權益保
障，並持續透過教育訓練提升員工技能，強化員工與公司共同成長之基礎。
G：(Governance)。大量科技致力於推動產品朝高規格、高自動化、高智能化
及完全整合化之方向發展，以貼近客戶需求，並與客戶共同開發新產品。此
外，公司持續投入自動化設備之研發，厚植核心技術能力，並持續開發進階
產品，以掌握市場先機。為確保制度有效運作，大量科技於企劃室下設企業
誠信經營小組，由總經理擔任召集人，負責誠信經營政策之推動與執行情形
之監督，並每年向董事會報告執行成果及防範不誠信行為之措施。大量科技
依董事會通過之「誠信經營守則」及相關內部規章，建立對於不誠信行為之
相關防範機制，並透過內部控制、檢舉制度及教育訓練等措施，確保誠信經
營政策之落實。 
公司簡介： 
大量的產主要產品應用於製造、半導體先進製程與先進封裝，以及光電、玻
璃基板與精密材料加工等領域。透過自主研發之控制器、應用軟體、視覺定
位、深度控制、運動控制及量測檢測技術，大量科技依客戶製程需求提供設
備及技術服務。 
PCB 設備：PCB 鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、邊緣滾塗、針塗等塗佈製程及
高速型內層銅深度量測機等。 
半導體設備：CMP Pad 即時量測監控設備、3D IC 先進封裝製程晶圓及晶片
量測設備、FOPLP 形貌量測設備、3DIC 先進封裝製程檢測設備、Flip Chip 
製程檢測設備、捲帶包裝晶片本體及外觀缺陷之檢查設備等、封裝製程自動
化設備等。 
大量 PCB 客戶的客戶包含台灣、中國的 PCB 廠；半導體設備客戶包含晶圓
代工廠、OSAT。  
高階背鑽設備的市場，主要同業為德國 Schmoll Maschinen。  
資料來源：大量年報。

4 群益投顧 
PCB 設備包含鑽孔機、成型機和塗膠設備。鑽孔設備也包含量測設備(TM3、
TM4 系列，應用於背鑽檢測)。  
大量有找外包廠商來增加產能： 
考量標準產品與高階產品，大量目前自有的月產能約 400 台，預計到 2027
年每月會達 500 台，大量會再增加外包的產能約 100~150 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：  
大量先進智能已經於 08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，
預計大量先進智能未來上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測(Metrology)、AOI 智慧檢
測 (Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板
級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、 CPO 等。

資料來源：大量法說簡報。 
2027 年 PCB 與半導體設備展望皆正向： 
PCB 設備 2027 年每月都有要交機，交期約 5~6 月；半導體設備 B/B ratio 維
持在 2 以上。 
大量預估 2027 年 CCD 成型機與鑽孔機將維持雙位數成長，TM3 完成階段
性任務後出貨將減少，TM4 出貨量預期會有大幅倍數成長。PCB 在手訂單
有達千台。

5 群益投顧 
大量有 CPO 相關設備，單價較一般半導體設備高： 
大量目前與北美 Tier 1 客戶合作，2026 年預計完成第一條產線的建置，量
產時程預計落在 3Q27。 2027 年設備需求量將倍數成長。 
由於該設備為整合光學前處理、檢測、後處理的一體機，結合了 3~4 項關鍵
技術，平均售價(ASP)約為一般晶圓檢測設備的 300~400%。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아쉬웠습니다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추정치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20 개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500 억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

Document 262: AI関連投資の失速は日本にどう波及するか ～日銀の金融システムレポートから考察する三つの経路～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI関連投資の失速は日本にどう波及するか ～日銀の金融システムレポートから考察する三つの経路～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026年10月 29日

1/8 
AI関連投資の失速は日本にどう波及するか 
～日銀の金融システムレポートから考察する三つの経路～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

（要旨） 
○ 日銀は 2026年 10月の金融システムレポートで、人工知能（AI）関連投資への期待が縮小し
た場合の金融安定上のリスクを分析した。本稿では同レポートに基づき日本へのリスクの波
及経路を整理したうえで、金融機関のストレス耐性を確認する。 
○ 米国の巨大IT企業による AI関連投資が急拡大している。これらの企業は従来の自己資金に
加え、社債発行などによる外部資金調達を増やしている。データセンター事業では、長期リ
ース契約を裏付けとした銀行融資や、プライベートファンドによる投融資が広がっている。
AI関連の旺盛な資金需要は経済成長につながる一方、特定分野へのリスクの集中と表裏一体
の関係にある。 
○ 日本への影響の波及経路として、銀行融資、金融市場、実体経済の三つが考えられる。日本
の大手行は海外のデータセンター事業者やファンドへの融資を増やしている。また、AI関連
株の下落をきっかけにヘッジファンドが投資ポジションを縮小すれば、日本の株式市場だけ
でなく、国債市場にも影響が及ぶ可能性がある。AI関連投資の減速によるアジア経済の悪化
も、日本の金融機関の信用コスト増加につながり得る。 
○ 日銀のストレステストでは、AI関連投資への期待の縮小とノンバンクによるショックの増幅
を想定しても、日本の金融機関は相応の耐性を備えているとの結果となった。一方、AI関連
企業の間では出資と将来の売上高の約束が結びついた循環的な取引がみられ、その影響はハ
イパースケーラーを通じて日本の大手行のデータセンター向け融資にも間接的に及び得る。
取引の連鎖全体を見る視点が必要だ。

1. はじめに 
日銀は10月23日、金融システムレポートを公表した。IMFが先に公表した国際金融安定性報告
書（GFSR）と同様、金融安定上のリスク要因として人工知能（AI）に焦点を当てた。AI関連企業
の成長期待を背景に、世界の株式市場では株価上昇が続き、データセンターなどへの設備投資も
拡大している。一方、投資規模が膨らむにつれて、巨額の投資に見合った収益が実現するかどう
かが金融市場の大きな関心事となっている。

### Page 2

2/8 
今回、注目したいのはAI関連投資と海外のノンバンク金融仲介機関（NBFI）の動向だ。AI関連
投資への期待が縮小した場合、海外ノンバンクの資産売却などを通じてショックが増幅され、日
本の金融市場にも波及する可能性を日銀は指摘している。本稿では、日銀の分析をもとに、AI関
連投資を支える資金調達の変化と、海外ノンバンクを通じた日本へのリスクの波及経路を整理し
たうえで、金融機関のストレス耐性を確認する。

2. 拡大するAI投資とノンバンクの役割 
AI関連投資は近年、大幅に増えている。日銀の図表によると、米国の巨大IT企業（ハイパース
ケーラー）5社の設備投資額は、2022年頃の四半期約 400億ドルから2026年 4～6月期には約
1,800億ドルと4倍以上に増加した（注1）。これらの企業は従来、潤沢な営業キャッシュフロー
をもとにデータセンター投資を行ってきた。ところが、足元では社債発行などによる外部資金調
達を増やすようになった。投資額の大きいデータセンターを例に見ていこう。 
ハイパースケーラーでは、自社でデータセンターを建設するだけでなく、外部事業者と長期リ
ース契約を結んで利用権を確保する動きも目立っている。5社の未開始のリース契約額は2026年
4～6月期に約1.1兆ドルに達した（資料 1、注1）。

資料 1 米ハイパースケーラー5社の設備投資と資金調達

（注）集計対象はAlphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、Oracleの 5社。①の「ネット借入・社債発行」と「ネット株式発行」は、プラス
が資金調達。直近は 2026年 4～6月期。数値は日本銀行の図表から筆者が読み取った概数。 
（出所）日本銀行「金融システムレポート」（2026年 10月号）図表Ⅴ-2-8（原資料はLSEG Workspace、SEC）をもとに第一ライフ資
産運用経済研究所作成

データセンターを建設・保有する事業者は、ハイパースケーラーとの長期リース契約を裏付け
に、銀行などから建設資金を調達している。銀行が融資を組成し、その貸出債権をプライベート
ファンドが購入する事例や、ファンドがデータセンター事業に直接出資する事例もある。こうし
て、AI関連投資を支える資金の出し手として、銀行に加えてプライベートファンドなどのNBFIの

### Page 3

3/8 
存在感が高まっている。 
日銀が集計した2023年1月から 2026年8月までに公表された米国のデータセンター案件で
は、資金調達額は合計約 1.6兆ドルに上る。このうち調達済みは約 0.2兆ドルにとどまり、約1.4
兆ドルが計画段階にある。また、案件数の約 2割でプライベートファンドがスポンサーとなって
いる（注2）。  
さらに、AI関連企業の間では、出資と将来の売上高が結びついた循環的な取引もみられる。IMF
が2026年10月に公表したGFSRによると、大手IT企業などが財務基盤の相対的に弱いAI企業に出
資し、出資を受けた企業が出資元などにサービスや半導体を発注する事例がある。IMFが確認した
2023年以降の14案件では、約 3,000億ドルの出資と約 6,500億ドルの債務調達が、約 1.4兆ドル
の将来の売上高の約束と結びついている。出資先の経営が悪化すれば、将来の売上高と投融資の
価値が同時に損なわれる可能性がある。 
こうした資金調達の拡大によって、AI関連投資と銀行、プライベートファンドなどの結びつき
が強まっている。AI関連投資が期待どおりの収益を生まなければ、データセンター事業者への融
資や出資を通じて、銀行やファンドに損失が生じる可能性がある。さらに、銀行はファンド自体
にも融資しており、ファンドの財務状況の悪化が銀行に波及する経路もある。日銀は、こうした
金融主体間の結びつきを通じて、ショックが金融システムに広がる可能性を警戒している。

3. 日本に及ぶ三つの波及経路 
AI関連投資の収益性に対する懸念の高まりや投資そのものの減速に直面した場合、日本の金融
システムにはどのような影響が及ぶのか。日銀の分析をもとに、①銀行融資、②金融市場、③実
体経済――の三つの波及経路からみていく。

（1） 銀行の融資を通じた経路 
日本の大手行は海外でデータセンター事業者やプライベートファンドへの融資を増やしてい
る。日銀によると、2026年 3月末時点で 3メガ行の海外貸出全体に占めるデータセンター向けの
割合は 3％、海外ファンド向けは9％だった。また、3メガ行の海外ファンド向け貸出残高は 12兆
円と、前年同月末から29.2％増加した。海外貸出全体に占める割合は現時点で限定的だが、収益
性の高さなどから注力分野となっている。データセンター事業者には賃料収入を特定の巨大IT企
業に依存するケースがあり、AI投資が期待どおりの収益を生まなければ、融資の返済能力にも影
響しかねない。また、技術革新によって高性能半導体などの設備が短期間で陳腐化するリスクも
ある。 
海外ファンド向け融資も注視すべきだろう。銀行はファンドに直接融資しているほか、ファン
ドが投資する企業や事業にも資金を供給している。ファンド向け貸出は、信用力の高い投資家の
出資約束を担保とする形式が多く、投資適格の比率は高い。ただ、日銀は、海外ファンドの運用
実態や資産価値を適時に把握するのは必ずしも容易ではないと指摘する。市場で大きなショック
が生じれば、ファンドが契約済みの融資枠から資金を引き出し、銀行の資金供給負担が増える可

### Page 4

4/8 
能性もある。

（2） 金融市場を通じた経路 
日本株の上昇は、AI関連需要の恩恵が相対的に大きい電機、精密、非鉄、機械などの業種がけ
ん引してきた。日銀は、AI関連投資に見合った収益拡大が実現しない場合、日本株にも調整圧力
が生じ得ると指摘しており、相応の株式を保有する金融機関にとってもリスクとなる。 
さらに、日銀によると、世界の株式市場に投資するヘッジファンドでは、AI関連銘柄の保有割
合が高まっている。マクロ戦略を採るヘッジファンドでは、個別株式の保有額に占める米大手ハ
イテク 7社の割合が、2019年の約14％から 2026年には約23％に上昇した（注 3）。こうしたファ
ンドのなかには、借り入れやデリバティブを活用して投資規模を拡大し、株式だけでなく国債な
どにも投資するものがある。AI関連株が急落すれば、損失の拡大や追加の証拠金請求などを受け
て、株式以外の投資ポジションも縮小する可能性がある。 
この影響が及び得る市場の一つが、日本の国債市場だ。ヘッジファンドを含む海外投資家は、
日本国債の現物と先物、金利スワップなどの価格差を利用した裁定取引を拡大させているとみら
れる。日銀によると、国債現物の対顧客取引に占める海外投資家の割合は、2015年の約 3割から
2025年には6割強に上昇した（注 4）。  
AI関連株の下落をきっかけにファンドがレバレッジを縮小すれば、国債関連のポジション解消
が進み、債券価格や金利の変動につながる可能性がある。日銀は、海外投資家による取引の増加
が市場の流動性や価格形成に寄与する場合がある一方、海外市場のショックが証拠金の請求など
をきっかけにポジション解消を引き起こし、日本の金融市場に影響を与えやすくなっている可能
性を指摘している。

（3） 実体経済を通じた経路 
日銀はストレステストで、ハイパースケーラーなどのAI関連投資が失速した場合、AI関連産業
の比重が高いアジア経済が大きく減速すると想定している。日本でも、情報通信機械、はん用・
生産用機械、電気機械などのAI関連産業を中心に経済が減速する。企業収益の悪化は、アジア向
けを中心とする貸出の信用コスト増加を通じて、金融機関に跳ね返る。 
AI関連投資を巡る三つの波及経路は相互に関連している。設備投資の減速による企業収益の悪
化は、株価の下落や信用リスクの上昇にもつながり得る。日本と海外NBFIの結びつきは、すでに
相当な規模に達している。日本の銀行による海外NBFIへの投融資残高は、2008年 3月末の8兆円
から 2026年6月末には126兆円に増加した。海外の投資ファンド等による日本の株式・債券への
投資残高も、同期間に111兆円から 408兆円へ拡大している（資料 2）。 このうち株式が 355兆円
を占め、債券は53兆円にとどまる（注5）。いずれもAI関連に限った金額ではないが、海外の金融
市場で発生したショックが日本に波及する経路の広がりを示している。

### Page 5

5/8

資料 2 日本の金融部門と海外ノンバンクの結びつき

（注）左図は国内銀行等による海外ノンバンクへの投融資残高。右図は、日銀がIMFの部門別対外証券投資データなどを用いて
試算した、海外主体による日本向け証券投資残高のうち「投資ファンド等」の値で、ディーラー・ブローカーやファイナンス会
社などを含む。海外ノンバンク全体の日本向け証券投資残高は 507兆円（2026年 6月末）。 
（出所）日本銀行「金融システムレポート」（2026年 10月号）図表Ⅴ-2-2（原資料はBIS、IMF、U.S. Department of the Treasury、
日本銀行）をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

4. 複合ショックにも耐性を示す日本の金融機関 
前章で整理した銀行融資、金融市場、実体経済の三つの波及経路に沿って、日本の金融機関へ
の影響をみていこう。日銀は銀行105行、信用金庫 247庫を対象に、2028年度末までの財務状況
を試算するマクロ・ストレステストを実施した。本稿で確認する「海外金利上昇シナリオplus」
では、AI関連投資の収益性に対する期待の縮小に加え、内外のNBFIによる資産売却などを通じて
金融市場の混乱が増幅される状況を想定した。 
まず、銀行融資を通じた波及経路を確認する。海外のデータセンター事業者やプライベートク
レジット（PC）ファンド向けの貸出が傷むというシナリオを置いた。両者に対する貸出の信用コ
スト率が最大約10ポイント悪化する想定だ。もっとも、国際統一基準行の信用コスト増加に占め
る海外ファンド・データセンター向けの寄与は限定的だった。 
金融市場を通じた経路では、株価や債券価格の大幅な下落を想定した。米国ではAI関連銘柄に
ITバブル崩壊後の下落率を当てはめるなどして、米国株価がベースラインから最大約 50％下落す
るとした。日本株も最大約 40％の下落を見込む。また、NBFIによるショックの増幅を織り込み、
米国のBBB格社債の信用スプレッドは最大約 1,100ベーシスポイント拡大する。日米の長期金利も
ベースラインより1.5ポイント上昇する。こうした株価と債券価格の同時下落によって、金融機
関の有価証券評価損益などが悪化する。また、海外金利の上昇に伴う外貨調達コストの増加が、
海外の資金利益を押し下げる。 
実体経済を通じた経路では、ハイパースケーラーなどのAI関連投資の失速によって、関連産業

### Page 6

6/8 
の比重が高いアジア経済が大きく減速すると想定した。日銀の試算では、アジアの実質GDPはベー
スラインから最大約7％下振れする。日本でも関連製造業を中心に企業収益が悪化し、銀行の信用
コスト増加につながる。今回のシナリオには原油高などの影響も含まれており、信用コストの増
加には国内向け貸出の寄与が大きい。 
こうした複合的なショックを想定しても、日銀の試算では金融機関の自己資本比率は全体とし
て規制水準を上回った。2028年度末の国際統一基準行の自己資本比率は、ベースラインの 12.8％
から 8.9％に低下する（資料 3）。

資料 3 マクロ・ストレステストにおける 2028年度末の自己資本比率

（注）対象は銀行 105行と信用金庫 247庫。「金融調整シナリオ」は、内外の金融市場や実体経済にリーマンショック並みの大幅
な調整が生じる想定。「海外金利上昇シナリオplus」は、内外金利の上昇、原油価格の上昇、AI関連投資の収益性に対する
期待の縮小などが同時に生じ、ノンバンク部門によるショックの増幅も織り込んだ想定。 
（出所）日本銀行「金融システムレポート」（2026年 10月号）図表Ⅵ-2-5、Ⅵ-2-7をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

この水準はリーマンショック並みの市場調整を想定した「金融調整シナリオ」の 10.1％を下回
る。今回のシナリオの影響の大きさが分かる。一方、国内基準行の銀行は 10.4％から 8.8％、信
用金庫は14.0％から 13.2％への低下にとどまった。国内基準行では、金融調整シナリオ（銀行
8.6％、信用金庫12.6％）の方が低下幅は大きい。 
日銀は、AI関連投資への期待の縮小などがNBFIを通じて増幅される複合的なショックに対して
も、「金融システムの安定性は全体として維持される」と評価している。ただ、試算結果は業態ご
との平均値で、個々の金融機関の自己資本比率にはばらつきがある点には留意が必要だろう。

5. おわりに 
日銀のストレステストでは、AI関連投資への期待の縮小やNBFIを通じたショックの増幅を織り
込んでも、日本の金融機関は全体として相応の耐性を備えているとの結果となった。一方、AI関

### Page 7

7/8 
連企業間の循環的な取引が、損失を増幅させる可能性にも注目したい。 
現在のAI関連企業の資金調達の特徴として、資金と売上が企業間で相互に結びつく関係があげ
られる。この循環的な取引を拙稿「AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書
から読み解く新たなリスク～」で指摘した。本稿の 2章でも述べた通り、大手企業が財務基盤の
相対的に弱いAI企業に出資し、出資先が見返りとして大手企業のサービスや半導体を将来購入す
ることを約束する仕組みだ。IMFのGFSRによると、約 6,500億ドルの債務のうち約3,200億ドルは
出資先が負っており、そこには出資元の保証付きの債務やオフバランスの債務も含まれる。出資
先の経営が悪化すれば、出資元の将来の売上高が減少すると同時に、出資や融資の価値も損なわ
れる可能性がある。IMFはこれを「誤方向リスク（wrong-way risk）」と呼び、循環的な取引が損
失を増幅するリスクを評価するよう監督当局に求めている。 
筆者は、日本の金融機関もこの構造と無縁ではないと考える。データセンター事業者は、ハイ
パースケーラーとの長期リース契約を裏付けに、銀行などから資金を調達している。IMFによる
と、循環的な取引の出資を主導しているのはハイパースケーラーだ。出資先のAI企業の経営が悪
化すれば、取引相手となるハイパースケーラーの将来の売上高が下振れし、データセンターの需
要見通しにも影響が及ぶ可能性がある。そうなれば、ハイパースケーラーとのリース契約を裏付
けとする日本の大手行のデータセンター向け融資も、間接的に影響を受ける可能性がある。表面
上は信用力の高いハイパースケーラーとの契約に見えても、その先に循環的な取引が組み込まれ
ていれば、リスクは見かけより高いのではないか。 
日本の金融機関のデータセンター向けやファンド向けの貸出は、現時点では海外貸出の一部に
とどまる。それでも、損失が特定の企業群にとどまらず、連鎖的に広がっていくリスクには目を
配っておきたい。AI関連投資が持続的な収益を生み出せるかに加え、企業間の出資と取引の結び
つきが、銀行やNBFIの資金供給を通じて金融システムにどう波及するのか。その把握には、個々
の融資先の信用力だけでなく、取引の連鎖全体を見る視点が必要になるだろう。

以 上

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth in September, while
the annual pace moved into contraction at –0.8%yr. The
result was well above both Westpac’s expectation for a
7.0%mth decline and the market expectation for a 2.0%mth
rise. Unit approvals continued to drive headline volatility,

surging in September, while detached dwellings, which
provide a better read on the underlying trend, recorded a
sizeable decline.
Note that most figures in the charts and table are presented on
a rolling-three month basis to smooth out monthly volatility.
Private detached dwelling approvals fell 5.3%mth in
September, unwinding August’s upwardly-revised 5.4%mth
rise (+1.7ppts). Recent resilience has kept the segment
elevated, with approvals remaining above 10,000 and the

annual pace at 9.6%yr. However, the quarterly profile provides
a clearer read on the underlying trend and suggests detached
dwelling approvals are beginning to respond to recent interest
rate increases (see here). Approvals rose just 0.9%qtr in
September vs 3.1%qtr in June and 5.9%qtr in March.
Private unit approvals remained volatile, rising 16.6%mth in
September following 21.7%mth decline in August. Strength
was led by high-rise approvals, while low-mid-rise approvals

recorded a more modest increase. Despite the monthly rise,
the annual pace fell further into contraction at –20.5%yr.
Among the states, NSW recorded the strongest result,
with private approvals rising 35.3%mth led by a surge in
private unit approvals. Queensland also recorded an increase
(+9.3%mth), while WA (–2.9%mth), SA (–3.5%mth) and
Victoria (–29.0%mth) all declined.
In value terms, residential building approvals fell 2.8%mth,
driven by a 3.3%mth decline in new residential approvals,

partially offset by a 0.8%mth rise in renovation activity.
Non-residential approvals once again reversed sharply, rising
45.9%mth, as the data centre-dominated ‘other commercial’
segment recorded an 8-fold increase on a non-seasonally
adjusted basis. The total value of building approvals rose
13.0%mth in September. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis,
the value of building approvals rose 8.3%mth overall, but
declined 3.0%mth when excluding the ‘other commercial’

segment.
September’s result reinforces the importance of looking
through headline volatility in unit approvals. While total
approvals rose in the month, detached dwelling approvals
slowed on a quarterly basis and fell across all states, pointing
to softer underlying momentum. The annual pace of total
approvals also moved into contraction for the first time
since January. Interest rate increases, the sharp downturn
in established housing markets and rising costs are likely to

weigh on further growth, with pessimistic consumer sentiment
an additional headwind (see here).

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cerebras大涨！Q3营收超预期，云业务激增272%，上调全年指引｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 3

### Page 1

Cerebras 三季度核⼼营收 2.3 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 70% ，⾼于市场预期的 2.15 亿美元；其
中，云服务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 272% ⾄ 1.45 亿美元。公司上调 2026 年全年核⼼营收指引，从此
前的 8.8 亿⾄ 8.9 亿美元上调⾄ 9.4 亿⾄ 9.6 亿美元，预计第四季度核⼼营收将达到 3.1 亿⾄
3.3 亿美元。 Cerebras 股价盘后上涨 16% 。
摘要：
AI 推理需求持续升温， AI 芯⽚公司 Cerebras 第三季度业绩超出市场预期，云业务收⼊成为增⻓
的核⼼引擎。公司不仅上调了 2026 年全年营收指引，还预计 2027 年营收将达到今年的三倍，显
⽰其对 AI 推理算⼒需求的增⻓前景持乐观态度。
当地时间 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇，Cerebras 公布 2026 年第三季度财报。公司当季核⼼营收达到 2.309 亿美
元，同⽐增⻓ 70% ，⾼于分析师平均预期的 2.147 亿美元；其中，云及其他服务收⼊同⽐增⻓
272% ⾄ 1.449 亿美元。

受云业务增⻓和客⼾需求推动，Cerebras 将 2026 年全年核⼼营收指引从此前的 8.8 亿⾄ 8.9 亿美
元上调⾄ 9.4 亿⾄ 9.6 亿美元。公司还预计，第四季度核⼼营收将达到 3.1 亿⾄ 3.3 亿美元，核⼼⽑
利率升⾄ 47% ⾄ 49% 。
订单储备⽅⾯，截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Cerebras 剩余履约义务（ RPO ）达到 261 亿美元。公司同时披
露，已经签约 700 兆⽡的数据中⼼容量，计划在 2027 年底前交付。
不过，强劲的营收增⻓尚未转化为 GAAP ⼝径下的盈利改善。第三季度公司净亏损扩⼤⾄ 1.612
亿美元，上年同期为 2220 万美元。随着公司加快芯⽚制造和数据中⼼扩张，股权激励、客⼾认
股权证摊销等项⽬也使 GAAP 与核⼼⼝径的经营表现出现明显差异。
财报公布后， Cerebras 股价在盘后交易中上涨 16% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

云业务收⼊激增，硬件销售并⾮唯⼀增⻓引擎
从收⼊结构来看， Cerebras 正在扩⼤云端算⼒服务的业务规模。第三季度，公司 GAAP ⼝径云及
其他服务收⼊达到 1.419 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 265% ；核⼼⼝径下，该项收⼊为 1.449 亿美元，同⽐
增⻓ 272% ，约占核⼼总营收的 63% 。
相⽐之下，硬件业务收⼊表现相对疲弱。第三季度 GAAP 硬件收⼊为 6888 万美元，低于去年同
期的 9679 万美元。按照核⼼⼝径计算，硬件收⼊为 8596 万美元，也低于去年同期的 9679 万美
元。
这⼀变化反映出 Cerebras 的收⼊结构正在向云服务倾斜。公司不再仅仅依赖出售 AI 芯⽚和计算
系统，还通过⾃营云平台及第三⽅云服务商提供推理算⼒，以扩⼤客⼾获取算⼒的渠道。
不过，硬件收⼊下降并不意味着公司正在主动放弃硬件销售。CEO Andrew Feldman 的表态
称，硬件销售受到交付时间不均衡的影响，⽽⾮公司战略转向云业务。
OpenAI 、微软和 Oracle 扩⼤合作，算⼒订单储备达 261 亿美元

⼤型客⼾合作正在成为 Cerebras 扩张的重要⽀撑。
Cerebras 与 OpenAI 达成了⼀项多年期、价值约 200 亿美元的算⼒供应协议。公司正在扩⼤芯⽚
供应规模，以满⾜相关协议及其他客⼾的算⼒需求。
微软已成为 Cerebras 的新客⼾，现有合作伙伴还包括 Oracle 和 OpenAI 。 Oracle 此前宣布，将
Cerebras 的⾼速 AI 推理能⼒整合进 Oracle Cloud Infrastructure ，相关服务预计于 2027 年上线。
订单储备⽅⾯，截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Cerebras 剩余履约义务（ RPO ）达到 261 亿美元。公司同时披
露，已经签约 700 兆⽡的数据中⼼容量，计划在 2027 年底前交付。
此外， Cerebras 还宣布与⼀家全球前三⼤超⼤规模云服务商达成新的云业务合作，但尚未披露
客⼾名称。公司表⽰，相关信息将在明年进⼀步公布。
这些合作意味着， Cerebras 正在通过直接销售、云平台和第三⽅云服务商等多种渠道扩⼤商业

覆盖范围。对于公司⽽⾔，能否按计划交付数据中⼼容量并将订单储备转化为实际收⼊，将成
为未来增⻓的重要观察指标。
上调全年指引，预计 2027 年营收增⻓⾄今年三倍
在第三季度业绩超预期后， Cerebras 进⼀步上调了全年业绩展望。
公司⽬前预计，2026 年核⼼营收将达到 9.4 亿⾄ 9.6 亿美元，⾼于此前预计的 8.8 亿⾄ 8.9 亿美元。
第四季度核⼼营收预计为 3.1 亿⾄ 3.3 亿美元，核⼼⽑利率预计达到 47% ⾄ 49% ，核⼼营业利润率
预计在 -1% ⾄ 1% 之间。
对于 2027 年， CEO Andrew Feldman 给出了更为积极的增⻓预期，预计公司营收将达到 2026
年的三倍。
为⽀撑这⼀⽬标， Cerebras 正在加快制造和基础设施扩张。公司预计，到 2026 年底，制造产能
将超过年初的 10 倍，并计划在 2027 年进⼀步增加⼯⼚和⽣产能⼒。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

图⽚
在资⾦⽅⾯，公司披露，截⾄第三季度末，现⾦、现⾦等价物、受限现⾦及短期投资合计达到
78 亿美元，另有 8.5 亿美元债务融资能⼒。
GAAP 亏损扩⼤，增⻓质量仍待检验
尽管营收和订单储备表现强劲， Cerebras 的盈利状况仍值得关注。
第三季度，公司 GAAP 净亏损达到 1.612 亿美元，较去年同期的 2220 万美元明显扩⼤； GAAP 营
业亏损达到 2.241 亿美元。相⽐之下，公司核⼼营业利润率为 -14.3% ，仍未实现调整后营业盈
利。
股权激励费⽤是两种财务⼝径出现明显差异的重要因素。第三季度，公司股权激励费⽤达到
1.551 亿美元，前三季度累计达到 5.417 亿美元。此外，客⼾认股权证摊销也对 GAAP 业绩产⽣影
响。
从⽑利率来看，第三季度 GAAP ⽑利率为 20% ，核⼼⽑利率为 41% 。公司预计第四季度核⼼⽑利

率将进⼀步升⾄ 47% ⾄ 49% ，但这⼀改善仍有待后续财报验证。
对 Cerebras ⽽⾔， AI 推理需求增⻓、云服务客⼾拓展和算⼒基础设施扩张，正在推动公司营收
快速增⻓。但在⾼额资本投⼊和股权激励费⽤的背景下，订单能否按期转化为收⼊，以及规模
扩张能否带来持续的盈利改善，仍是投资者需要关注的问题。

Document 305: CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline

Source PDF: all report pdf/CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

The Bank Board increased interest rates to 4% in a close call,
and we see valid arguments for both positions. Second-round
eﬀects will push inﬂation above the upper bound of the target
corridor for some time, while dwindling economic activity
warrants lots of caution. We see neither hikes nor cuts as the
most likely outcome for quite some time
David Havrlant
The Czech National Bank's decision to raise rates on 5 November was a close
call
Assessing the impact of Hormuz

The Czech National Bank increased the policy rate by 25bp to 4% in
a split vote on 5 November, with Governor Ales Michl and board
members Jan Kubicek, Jan Prochazka and Jakub Seidler voting for
a hike. Meanwhile, Deputy Governors Eva Zamrazilova and Jan Frait,
along with board member Karina Kubelkova, favoured interest rate
stability. We assess the current situation and the risks to the
outlook as complex and see relevant justiﬁcation for both options,

depending on whether your concerns are more on the side of

### Page 2

second-round inﬂationary eﬀects or on the side of disappointing
economic activity and the consequences for consumption.
The Bank Board assessed overall risks as pro-inﬂationary. There
was an agreement that the secondary impacts of the energy shock
have not been signiﬁcantly reﬂected in domestic inﬂation so far.
However, as the Hormuz conﬂict becomes more protracted, the
likelihood of tangible second-round eﬀects increases. Jakub Seidler
noted that global energy prices have been elevated for too long, so

it would be diﬃcult to avoid a pass-through to other segments of
consumer prices. Deputy Governor Zamrazilova emphasised that
much depends on further developments in the Middle Eastern
conﬂict. Should tensions ease in the near future, a rate hike at the
November meeting would not be necessary. The key question is
whether the conﬂict will eventually pass or become the new
normal, with the latter implying persistently high energy costs.
Deputy Governor Frait stated that energy prices remaining at these

levels for such an extended period could have a negative impact on
demand and economic activity, mainly by reducing the real
incomes of households and companies. The current negative
supply shock has hit the Czech economy from a favourable starting
position, but the factors that helped keep inﬂation close to the
target this year are about to fade. The increase in longer-term rates
contributes to the restrictive stance of monetary policy, and implies

some softening in domestic lending activity. The board members
agreed that this is a key factor reducing the need for further
tightening for the time being.
To rise or not to rise?
However, as Jan Kubicek noted, core inﬂation is not slowing down
as previously assumed and is even slightly increasing. This
represents a fundamental concern, especially in combination with
the disappointing real growth performance. That said, producer
prices have embarked on an upward trend and may put further

pressure on the core rate. Jakub Seidler and Karina Kubelkova
emphasised that the renewed price increases in the energy and
food segment are usually the key drivers when it comes to inﬂation

### Page 3

expectations, and the ﬁrst signs of an increase are already visible.
And we all know that unanchored inﬂation expectations are one of
the things that keep central bankers awake at night.
Producer prices will drive consumer prices
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
En gros, among the main reasons for a rate hike in November were:
i) persistent core inﬂation, ii) still robust wage dynamics and iii)
second-round eﬀects expected to bite soon. Among the main

reasons for staying on hold and evaluating were: i) secondary
inﬂationary impacts are so far limited, ii) long-term rates deliver
restriction, iii) disappointing economic activity.
We see the arguments in both directions as valid, which made the
November decision relatively complex and was reﬂected in the
close vote. Time will tell whether the second-round eﬀects that we
believe are just kicking in will prove signiﬁcant without weighing too
heavily on economic activity. Or whether the deteriorating real

purchasing power and dwindling real wage growth could lead to
weaker household consumption, which would in turn put
downward pressure on inﬂation. Both eﬀects are certainly
interconnected, which only underscores the beauty of
macroeconomics, and the formidable power of prices to clear the
market.

### Page 4

Punchy inﬂation across price segments
When it comes to our inﬂation forecast, we deﬁnitely see pro-
inﬂationary pressures when looking ahead. We expect annual
producer price growth to peak at around 7% in industry early next
year and at around 12% in agriculture mid-next year. At the same
time, the annual pace of import prices already quickened to above
8% in September. Those are not low growth rates, and all are going
to feed into consumer prices sooner or later. At the same time, the

koruna continues to weaken against the dollar, making imports
pricier. With all that on board, we see the yearly average of inﬂation
picking up from 2.1% this year to 3.2% next, reaching a peak of just
below 4% in February.
Inﬂation to hover above the target for
longer
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
The most pronounced uncertainty lies with January’s gain in
regulated prices. We have already collected information from
distributors and factor in a 3.5% increase in electricity prices, a

6.5% increase in natural gas prices, an 8% increase in heating
prices and a 6% rise in water charges. Nonetheless, not all
distributors will apply the same increase and not all households will
be aﬀected equally. Based on our best estimate, we expect a 3%
monthly increase in regulated prices in January, resulting in a 3.8%

### Page 5

year-on-year gain. However, should global oil and European natural
gas prices remain elevated, we expect further increases throughout
the year, lifting average regulated price growth to 4.5% next year.
This ﬁgure is still somewhat below the CNB's 5.6% forecast, which
seems to be at the upper bound of what we currently deem
plausible.
Regulated and food prices turn pro-
inﬂationary
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
Food prices are set to shift from an anti-inﬂationary position this

year to an inﬂationary one, averaging just below 3% next year, but
peaking at around 4%. That said, we sense quite some upward risks
here, especially should energy prices stay elevated for longer, which
would propel fertiliser prices and transport costs.
Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline
When it comes to core inﬂation, two opposing forces are set for a
duel: second-round eﬀects, coupled with rising import prices,
against a softening housing market and crumbling real purchasing

power. Timing is of the essence. We expect the former to strike ﬁrst,
but the latter to respond without much hesitation. For now, we see
the forces as roughly balanced, keeping average core inﬂation just
below 3% next year.

### Page 6

Consumption could slow tangibly along with
real wages
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
So yes, upbeat inﬂation across price segments warrants restrictive
monetary policy. Meanwhile, weakening economic activity
warrants a lot of caution when it comes to any future rate hikes.
The thing is, we see real GDP growth averaging just 1.7% this year
and lifting oﬀ only gradually to 2.1% next year. Meanwhile, we see
household consumption slowing tangibly to 1.8% next year from

2.5% this year. Indeed, we are likely positioned at the lower end of
the forecast range, yet we take the view that a slowdown in real
wage growth will eventually take its toll. Therefore, we see neither
further hikes nor cuts when looking way ahead, with the 4% policy
rate settling in as the new normal.

It would be a diﬀerent story, however, should we see another round
of escalation in the current conﬂicts, pushing Brent crude back

towards US$120/bbl. In that case, the path to a 4.5% CNB base rate
is pretty straightforward, if it pleases. If this were to happen, the
EUR/CZK would likely lend a helping hand to mitigate the imported
inﬂationary pressures from the eurozone, as the European Central
Bank seems constrained in this respect, not least by elevated French
bond yields. In contrast, should we slip into an episode of stagnant
growth in Europe for whatever reason, our hypothesis of a 4%

nominal interest rate becoming the new normal would start to look
a little shakier, at least temporarily.

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因，
貢獻整體CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性，需求面
通膨略有提高，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在4%以上維持更久。受
到油價高盤、通膨僵固，與財政惡化、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大、
歐元走弱，美元轉強，商品、黃金承壓。法
國政治不確定性讓歐股修正、法股最疲，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高，增加企業借貸成本
與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現。根據FactSet數據
顯示，標普500指數第三季獲利成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI需求帶動， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力。高盛指出，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面。隨財報
數據陸續公布，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現，並維持正向財測，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐

評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張

投資焦點
► 醫療保健、醫療服務與生物技術等行業，在
景氣不好時，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天，股市波動風險上升，觀察
過去當VIX恐慌指數上揚時，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑，部分公司估值偏低。當
臨床數據改善、或導入AI篩選資料與藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值，帶動整體產業估值回升。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場需求，
創新藥物(如瘦瘦針)讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場2030年銷售額預估上調15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。
醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健

市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響，由於商業雇主
贊助保險(ESI)和平價醫療法案(ACA)的自付
額增加，以及最高自付費用要求，較去年同
期提高，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限)來擴大利潤空間，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險(ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去20 年來的最高水準。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間，殖利率偏高環境使短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等

匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高，使金價短期承壓，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 428: US Midterms_ The cost of living is now a pivotal issue (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Midterms_ The cost of living is now a pivotal issue (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026EDITORIAL
US MIDTERMS: THE COST OF LIVING IS NOW A PIVOTAL ISSUE
1 AP-NORC, October 2026.
2 Jared Bernstein, Daniel Posthumus, ‘The way we were: Price-level shocks and consumers’ memories’, SIEPR, May 2026.
3 72% of registered voters rank ending the war with Iran as one of the top priorities for the next Congress, including 60% of Republicans (AP-NORC, October 2026).

4 Oleg Korenok, David Munro, Jiayi Chen, ‘Inflation and attention thresholds’, GLO Discussion Paper Series, No. 1175, Global Labour Organization, 2022.
5 Rajashri Chakrabarti, Thu Pham, Beck Pierce, Maxim L. Pinkovskiy, ‘Tracking the K-shaped economy: who’s driving spending?’, Federal Reserve Bank of New York, Liberty
Street Economics, 1st May 2026.
6 The year 2020 is excluded due to the disruptions caused by lockdowns.
In the United States, growth is holding up, unemployment remains low and consumer spending shows no sign of slowing.

On paper, that should flatter the president’s party ahead of the midterms. Yet, Republicans could lose control of Congress.
The rise in the cost of living, which weighs on households far more than macroeconomic aggregates suggest, could be one
of the reasons. On top of persistent inflation, rekindled by the war in Iran, there is also a slowdown in wage growth and
rising housing costs. The election could hinge on affordability, yet whichever party wins will lack the means to address it.

A change in the majority in Congress will nonetheless have an impact on the country’s economy.
On 3 rd November, Americans will elect a new Congress for the next
two years. With Republicans currently in control of the White House,
the House of Representatives and the Senate, the balance of power in
Washington could shift. Polls suggest that the House, at least, could
swing to the Democrats. Beyond the new balance of power, the vote
will reflect how voters view the way the country is being run. Here, we

focus on the cost of living.
INFLATION: THE INCUMBENTS’ BURDEN
The incumbent party goes into the election with two handicaps.
The first is structural: no matter who holds the White House, the pre -
sident’s party almost always loses seats in midterm elections. The
second stems from the combination of robust but uneven growth
and persistent inflation, which is undermining household confidence.
Pre-election polls1 confirm this assessment: the economy and the cost-

of-living top voters’ worries.
Inflation has overshot the Fed’s target every year since 2021. Prices are
up 28% over five years: 31% for housing (not including mortgage rates,
which have more than doubled), and 29% for food. This shock is felt all
the more keenly as it is the most severe in forty years and follows on
from decades of low inflation2. Furthermore, it hits the lowest-income
households the hardest. In addition, the election comes after an ener-

gy-driven rise in inflation. Energy accounts for the entire gap between
the latest CPI reading (3.7% y/y in September) and the last figure re -
corded before the conflict in the Middle East started (2.4% y/y).
Fuel has made things worse. The rise in the price of a gallon (46%
between February and October) caused by the Iran war — which is
unpopular even amongst Republican voters3 — is an aggravating factor.
In 2026, inflation only slightly crossed the ‘attention threshold’, the

3.6%y/y rate beyond which households start to regard it as problema -
tic4. Fuel is a recurring, unavoidable expense whose price is directly
observable, unlike many items in the CPI basket. This results in a subs-
tantial gap between perceived inflation and measured inflation.
Inflation must be viewed alongside the other drivers of households’
well-being. Today, wages, payrolls and the housing market are moving
the wrong way for most Americans.
IT'S NOT JUST PRICES

The 2022 midterms suggest caution in reading the polls. Democrats
defied expectations of a ‘Republican wave’, retaining control of the Se-
nate and losing the House by just a few seats, even though inflation
averaged 8% that year. This reminds us that inflation must be viewed
alongside the other drivers of households’ well-being. Indeed, how
people experience the cost of living depends on the interplay between
prices, wages and housing. In 2022, wages, payrolls and the housing

market were all growing faster than in 2026. Today, all three are mo -
ving the wrong way for most Americans.
Wage growth is slowing just as inflation picks up again. In September
2026, average hourly earnings were up 3% y/y in nominal terms, the
slowest rate since 2018, and now below inflation. Real wage earnings
are therefore falling (-0.7% y/y in September, with six out of nine mon-
ths showing a decline in 2026). The rise in the cost of living is thus

compounded by a net loss of purchasing power. The Atlanta Fed reports
that wage growth is lower for the bottom quartile of earners (the 25%
of employees with the lowest pay) than for the top quartile (the 25%
with the highest pay): 3.8% y/y vs. 4.4% on average since late 2024,
following 10 years in which the gap favoured the bottom quartile. Top
earners also benefit most from the wealth effect linked to stock mar -
ket valuations. This explains the resilience of aggregate consumption.

However, this rests on a relatively narrow base, mostly households
earning more than USD 125,000/year5. Meanwhile, a falling saving rate
(3.8% in September 2026, down 1.4pp y/y) suggests households are
drawing on their savings to offset price rises. A large share of the po -
pulation is therefore excluded from the growth that has been observed.
Beyond wages, there is the question of employment prospects.
The unemployment rate remains low and consistent with full employ-

ment. Yet the labour market looks stagnant: low firing despite repeated
shocks and low hiring despite solid growth. Payroll growth (an average
of 68,000 per month since January) is well below that of the previous
decade (nearly 200,000 per month between 2011 and 2019 6). Com -
bined with a workforce that is no longer growing due to an ageing
population and a restrictive immigration policy, this is enough to keep
the unemployment rate low and the labour market looking healthy.

### Page 2

4
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
But households are likely to be suffering from a lack of opportunities,
and consequently from a lack of prospects for wage growth that would
help them to increase their purchasing power. The Conference Board’s
labour differential, a gauge of perceived job availability, says as much:
outside the pandemic, it is at its lowest since 2015.
Housing is the area that has deteriorated the most. The rise in

mortgage rates that began in 2022, from under 4% to over 7%, has
dampened the property market. This is true on the demand side, as
expected, but also on the supply side. The sharp rise in interest rates
is deterring homeowners with low-interest mortgages from selling, as
they would have to borrow again at current rates. This ‘lock-in effect’
prevents prices from falling despite weaker demand. The lock-in effect
is also affecting tenants, as the decline in the availability of housing is

driving up rents. Housing lies at the heart of the disconnect between
the macroeconomic picture and voters’ perception of it, as they face a
multifaceted rise in the cost of living. That rise is also driven by credit:
monthly repayments on a home of the same value have risen shar -
ply. This additional cost, which inflation figures do not capture7, erodes
households’ purchasing power in the property market.
A CONGRESS POWERLESS ON THE COST OF LIVING

The next Congress will, in all likelihood, have little room for ma -
noeuvre. Neither party is within reach of the thresholds required to
override the filibuster (which allows 41 out of 100 senators to block
a bill from coming to a vote) and the presidential veto (two-thirds of
both chambers).
Affordability is likely to be a key issue in the election, but the winners
will lack the tools to address it. The Democrats’ agenda relies notably
on the reintroduction of schemes that were abolished or scaled back

under the current administration (Affordable Care Act tax credits, Me -
dicaid). Even if Congress passed them, they would be subject to a pre-
sidential veto. There is no question of direct support for households or
a restrictive fiscal policy.
Whether inflation falls will depend on two factors: how the conflict in
Iran unfolds and the effectiveness of monetary policy. The labour mar-
ket should hold its current balance: robust growth (see our EcoFlash)

is supporting demand, whilst failing to alleviate supply-side pressures,
and the unemployment rate could continue to fall. Household senti -
ment will continue to depend on how prices develop. As for housing,
it is unlikely to improve in the short term. With the 10-year Treasury
yield still at 4.8% at the end of 2027 (BNP Paribas forecast), 30-year
mortgage rates would, at best, remain around 7%.
THE OTHER ECONOMIC STAKES OF THE VOTE
Historically, divided governments have brought legislative stability,

which is viewed favourably by the markets. But polarisation has in -
creased in recent years and is likely to feature prominently in the bud-
get vote and the debt-ceiling debate. The debt limit, in particular, could
be reached as early as Q1 2027, depending on the Treasury’s borrowing
requirements. The current administration, meanwhile, has taken major
decisions (on tariffs, immigration and Iran) without involving Congress,
a practice that is likely to continue.

7 Marijn A. Bolhuis, Judd N. L. Cramer, Karl Oskar Schulz, Lawrence H. Summers, ‘The cost of money is part of the cost of living: new evidence on the consumer sentiment
anomaly’, NBER Working Paper, No. 32163, 2024.
Risks to the deficit remain tilted to the upside. According to our base -
line scenario, it is expected to remain stable at 6.3% of GDP in 2027,
but the post-election balance of power could add to the cost of the war
in Iran and rising debt-service costs. In particular, interests could align

in favour of increased spending should the Democrats gain control of
both houses of Congress. Avoiding, or ending, a shutdown could require
a trade-off involving mutual concessions from the presidency on key,
popular aspects of the Democratic agenda (such as healthcare), and
from the Democrats on defence.
The AI sector is exposed to the vote. The economic stakes are high
given how much the associated investment cycle and the wealth effect
contribute to US growth. However, at this stage, the risk is not legisla-

tive. As with the cost of living, sweeping legislation is unlikely, all the
more so with a president holding a pro-AI stance, and who holds the
right of veto. The risk lies rather in sentiment and further out. A Demo-
cratic sweep could lead to increased congressional activism on regu -
lation, on concentration in the AI industry, and on the knock-on effects
on the cost of living (data centres and electricity) and on jobs. Putting
these issues on the agenda could create a climate conducive to tighter

regulation in the medium term and weigh on the sector’s valuations.
Furthermore, the mid-term elections will see 36 governors’ seats up
for election. These governors have extensive powers (regarding autho-
risations and incentives) when it comes to the establishment of data
centres in their states.
Finally, the midterms will matter for monetary policy. The Senate
confirms, by simple majority, the president’s nominees to the Fed’s
Board of Governors. Consequently, should the Senate come under De -

mocratic control, President Trump would need a consensus pick to re-
place Governor Powell from 2028 — or to fill any other seat that might
become vacant during the next Congress.
*****
Whatever the outcome of the election, disinflation and, more broadly,
relief from the cost of living, depend less on Congress than on the
White House and, to a lesser extent, the Fed. The key factor lies in the
resolution of the conflict in Iran, coupled with an effective monetary

policy to enable a sustainable return to price stability.

Report source: 国信证券

### Page 1

专题研究报告
海外养老金 ESG 投资实践与经验借鉴
核心观点 发展研究·专题研究
证券分析师：陈锐
0755-81981573
chenrui1@guosen.com.cn
S0980516110001
养老金是海外ESG投资的先行者与稳定器，这一格局由资产属性、制度环境
与统计口径共同塑造。长期、避险、公共三重属性使ESG因素纳入投资决策
成为负债管理的内生需求；在受托责任框架下，纳入ESG因素是履行审慎义
务的工具，而非外加约束。2013-2018年的集中制度供给将ESG纳入规则文
本，同期及此后，欧盟IORP II与SFDR逐步形成审慎管理和强制披露的监
管底座。资产所有者话语权与投资者协作机制持续增强，衡量ESG投资发展
的标准也由规模叙事转向可核验披露。2019年以来，深化与回摆并存，部分
法域强化ESG义务，披露趋向条例化、独立报告与统一分类；与此同时，欧
盟以“综合简化方案”压缩披露细则，美国联邦规则反复调整。相关回摆主
要发生在披露颗粒度与政治表态层面，尚未触及受托义务的存废。

北美养老金ESG实践的两条主线是去标签化与承诺重估，前者主要由政治压
力驱动，后者主要由法律风险与核算成本的重估驱动。美国经验表明，去标
签化并不意味着削弱气候投资，而是淡化ESG标签，将战略锚定到可核验的
投资行动与指标。CalPERS将气候解决方案投资额目标提高至2030年1000
亿美元，CalSTRS则提高低碳目标指数在内部管理公开股票组合中的权重。
加拿大的分化体现为承诺重估，CPPIB于2025年5月放弃2050年净零承诺
后，于同年10月被四名缴费人以未尽受托义务为由起诉；OTPP于2026年2
月撤回净零承诺并重构脱碳战略；LaCaisse虽保留2050年碳中和承诺，但
其主要执行工具是2030年4000亿加元转型脱碳资产目标及煤、油业务排除，
两类指标均可逐年核验。总体来看，北美机构并未退出气候投资，调整主要
集中于承诺结构、核算口径和标签表述；从案例看，越接近可核查出资事实
的承诺，相对更具存续能力。
欧洲的路径是制度先行，对欧盟样本而言，统一的是审慎管理与披露底线，

分化的是执行工具。欧盟以硬法将ESG纳入审慎人原则与强制披露，划定“必
须考虑、必须披露”的底线，并将转型风险评估与情景分析纳入资产负债管
理。执行层面，FRR将管理合同的脱碳目标与100%符合法国能源转型法的授
权组合绑定，ABP以行业碳预算和化石燃料撤资作硬性门槛，ATP以ESG整
合覆盖近全部组合、以道德排除处理尾部风险。与欧盟样本不同，挪威GPFG
依托本国立法授权与独立伦理委员会，以观察和排除清单管理伦理风险，以
对话和投票推动持股公司改善，形成撤资与尽责管理并重的治理机制。最新
变化来自机制自身，GPFG运行逾十年的伦理框架进入全面审查，新增排除与
观察决定已暂停，改革方向仍待政府与议会决定。这说明成熟机制也需根据
投资绩效、实施成本与治理约束进行再校准。
东亚的共同点是制度先行、工具渐次补位，并以参与而非撤资为主要手段。
日本GPIF被动投资占比超八成，委外比例超九成，因而主要通过指数编制
标准与委外管理链条传导ESG要求，其中指数化整合最具特色；在这一机制
下，GPIF不直接逐股筛选或撤资，而将ESG因子的选股与加权交由指数方法

完成；近年的“最适化”重构收缩的是配置规模而非方法，机制经收益再验
证后延续。韩国NPS同样是“条文先行、工具后补”，法条长期停留于“可
以考虑”的选择性条款，执行端缺乏纪律，ESG及气候类股东提案支持率低
于部分欧美同业；近年修法将责任投资原则适用范围扩至替代投资，但未改
变“可以考虑”的措辞，同时启动投票权外包改革试点，以弥补执行缺口，
2026年11月11日

### Page 2

管理、指数和委外治理上。这种自上而下推动、渐进试点、参与优先且保留
纠错空间的路径，对我国更具直接参照价值。
海外养老金ESG投资实践及其对我国的启示可归纳为四个维度。策略上，ESG
整合应作为基准策略，参与优先于撤资；部分机构放弃或重构全组合净零承
诺，表明承诺设计应更加重视可核查的投资额、转型资产与强度指标，我国
负面筛选则应限于共识类目，并辅以成本测算。委外维度，ESG要求可通过
管理人选聘、评分与费率结构传导至管理人链条。指标维度，应推进E/S/G
本土化适配，将本土评级、期望分数、披露率评分与碳核算用于委外评估和
股东对话，并与上市公司可持续披露制度同步迭代。制度维度，我国现状是
“条文未写、理念先行”，社保基金和基本养老保险基金现行主要投资规章
尚未写入ESG条款，而机构年报已采用责任投资表述；由于社保基金约七成
资产委托外部管理，理念主要经由委外链条传导。立法顺序宜先写入“可以
考虑”式条款，再升格为准义务性标准化要求，条件成熟时将ESG纳入审慎
人原则，并辅以主管部门目标文件-机构方针的行政通道，同时修订投资政

策、委托指引及委外考核，避免条款停留于原则表态。
风险提示：海外市场风险波动，政策效果不及预期。
